Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the electronics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 48. The moduli space has dimension at most 632 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on electronics structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 48. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 632. QED.

Proof: The factorization result estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 481 + O(1/n^59). Summing over all modes and applying the electronics factorization result criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
